### 1. user (2026-06-21T19:01:19.426Z)

An adversarial translation audit of the shared i18n catalogs (repo-root `/locales/kn/common.json` and `/locales/hi/common.json`) surfaced real quality bugs in the **SPA console** translations (the B5/B6 Kannada/Hindi work — AI-drafted, native-review-pending). These are in the non-`site` namespaces (`app.*`, `bulk.*`, `grid.*`, `footer.*`, `result.*`, `ingest.*`, `errors.*`); the `site.*` marketing namespace is fine (A3). The en source of truth is `/locales/en/common.json`. Scope this as a focused "native-review pass on the kn/hi SPA console catalog" PR.

CRITICAL (correctness):
- Hindi transliterates the brand "KhelSutra" → "खेलसूत्र" in `ingest.intro`, `ingest.reassure`, `ingest.fileHint` (check ALL hi keys for this). Brand MUST stay Latin "KhelSutra".
- `footer.floorSomeRest` (both kn + hi): leaks an internal `min_shot_count` code token (kn even wraps it in a `<code>` tag) and the word "engine". The en is plain "(they're too short)". Fix kn → "…(ಅವು ತುಂಬಾ ಚಿಕ್ಕದಾಗಿವೆ)." and hi → "…(वे बहुत छोटी हैं)।", dropping the token/tag.

JARGON LEAK (en deliberately avoids "engine"/"server"; both languages leak it):
- `footer.floorOne`, `footer.floorAll`: kn/hi say "engine (ಎಂಜಿನ್/इंजन) will skip it"; en says "it would be skipped" (no engine). Rephrase passively without "engine".
- `errors.networkError`: kn "ಎಂಜಿನ್ ತಲುಪಲಾಗುತ್ತಿಲ್ಲ" / hi "इंजन तक नहीं पहुँच…" — en is "Can't reach KhelSutra. Is it running?" Use the brand, not "engine".
- `ingest.intro` (kn + hi) and `ingest.reassure` (kn says "ಸರ್ವರ್‌ನಲ್ಲಿ", hi "सर्वर पर"): add bucket/engine/server clauses absent from en. Match en's plainer meaning.

HINDI FLUENCY (over-Sanskritized / stiff for non-tech coaches/parents):
- `app.badge`, `app.intro` (awkward calque), `grid.shots`/`grid.shotsTitle` ("प्रयोगात्मक"/"विकासाधीन"), `grid.shotsConfidence` ("विश्वास" for confidence), `query.support`, `result.dismiss` ("खारिज करें" → "बंद करें"/"हटाएँ").

KANNADA:
- `bulk.invert`: "ತಿರುಗಿಸಿ" means rotate, not invert-selection → use "ಆಯ್ಕೆ ಬದಲಿಸಿ".
- `result.dismiss`: "ವಜಾಗೊಳಿಸಿ" is legalistic → "ಮುಚ್ಚಿ".

Keep ALL `<b>`/ICU `{count, plural, …}`/`{min}`/`{count}` placeholders and `<x>`/`<i>` tags intact. After editing, run `cd web && npm ci && npm run typecheck && npm test` (the hygiene key-parity + placeholder-parity + es 3-form tests must stay green) and the SPA i18n render tests. Branch from origin/master, one PR, merge on green CI. Tag the catalogs "AI-draft + adversarial-review pass; native review still pending."

### 4. user (2026-06-21T19:01:25.323Z)

1	---
2	name: shared-checkout-concurrency
3	description: khelsutra is a shared single checkout with rapid concurrent master churn; isolate repo-writing work in a git worktree
4	metadata: 
5	  node_type: memory
6	  type: project
7	  originSessionId: dca1ff63-3aec-42ab-897f-41970003f4e1
8	---
9	
10	The `khelsutra` repo is worked by **multiple concurrent sessions on ONE shared working checkout** (`C:/Users/avidu/Projects/khelsutra`). During the 2026-06-21 B3 session, master advanced **5+ times** (#67→#68→#69→#71→#70) within ~1 hour, and a sibling **A9** session had **uncommitted catalog WIP sitting in the shared working tree** that carried across a `git checkout`.
11	
12	**Why:** the owner runs several parallel "slates"; the shared tree + current branch can shift under you between two commands, and sibling WIP must never be clobbered (a past incident had a squash-merge silently absorb sibling work).
13	
14	**How to apply (for any repo-writing task here):**
15	- **Don't work in the shared checkout when a sibling may be active.** Create an isolated worktree: `git worktree add <path> -b <branch> origin/master` (or check out an existing branch). Run `npm ci` per package there (web/ + site/ each have their own `node_modules`; not shared across worktrees). Clean up after merge: `git worktree remove <path> --force`.
16	- **Always branch from `origin/master`** and re-verify `git log origin/master..HEAD` shows ONLY your commits before opening a PR.
17	- **Stage explicit paths**, never `git add -A`.
18	- **Expect master to move mid-task.** If your PR shows `mergeable=false` but `git merge-base --is-ancestor origin/master HEAD` is true, GitHub's mergeability is just **stale** — collapse to one clean commit on top of latest `origin/master` (`git reset --soft origin/master` + recommit) and `git push --force-with-lease` to force a fresh recompute (this also triggers the `test` workflow, which GitHub **skips while a PR is CONFLICTING**).
19	
20	See [[catalog-home-and-i18n-layout]] for the i18n-specific contention (catalog files).
21

### 5. user (2026-06-21T19:01:26.271Z)

1	---
2	name: catalog-home-and-i18n-layout
3	description: "i18n catalogs live at repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME); SPA imports them, site mirrors them via a copy step"
4	metadata: 
5	  node_type: memory
6	  type: reference
7	  originSessionId: dca1ff63-3aec-42ab-897f-41970003f4e1
8	---
9	
10	After B3 (#70, 2026-06-21), the localization catalogs are the **single source of truth at repo-root `/locales/{en,kn,hi,es,da,id}/common.json`** (D-CATALOG-HOME). Both surfaces consume them:
11	
12	- **SPA (`web/`):** `web/src/i18n/index.ts` imports `../../../locales/<lng>/common.json` directly. Vite resolves the workspace root via the repo `.git` marker, so dev/build/vitest all read the parent-dir JSON. `web/src/i18n/hygiene.test.ts` reads `/locales` for key-parity + placeholder-parity (+ an es 3-form plural check). Adding a key to en means adding it to ALL locales or these tests fail.
13	- **Marketing site (`site/`, dependency-free):** `site/scripts/copy-locales.mjs` mirrors `/locales` → `site/public/locales/` (`npm run copy:locales`; `predeploy` hook). The committed copy is what ships; `site/src/locales-drift.test.js` fails CI if it drifts. The shim `site/public/i18n.js` (vanilla `t()`/`data-i18n`, ICU-lite via `Intl.PluralRules`) fetches those at runtime; `server.js` does Accept-Language negotiation (cookie→?lang→Accept-Language→en) and stamps `<html lang>`.
14	
15	**Negotiation contract (both surfaces, identical):** persisted (`localStorage`/cookie key `khelsutra.lang`) → `?lang=` → navigator/Accept-Language → `en`; a missing key degrades to en, never a raw key.
16	
17	**Marketing content is en-only so far (B3):** the `site.*` namespace in the catalogs is authored in en; non-en `site.*` values are en seeds **pending translation (tracker A3)**. The SPA console strings (app/query/grid/footer/…) ARE translated for kn/hi/es/da/id. Because the SPA bundles the whole `common.json`, it also ships the (unused) `site.*` keys — negligible.
18	
19	Catalog files are a **contention hotspot** across i18n PRs — see [[shared-checkout-concurrency]].
20

1	// CI guardrails that keep i18n "by design" (FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md §4):
2	//   1. KEY-PARITY — every locale catalog has EXACTLY the `en` key set (the extensibility proof:
3	//      drop a stub locale and this lists every gap). Trivially green while only `en` exists; it
4	//      starts enforcing the moment a kn/hi catalog lands (B5/B6).
5	//   2. NO-BARE-STRINGS (attribute level, v1) — user-facing component attributes (placeholder /
6	//      aria-label / title) must route through t(), never a hardcoded literal. A curated start per
7	//      the doc; an AST-level sweep of JSX text can extend it later.
8	import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
9	import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
10	import { join } from "node:path";
11	
12	// Catalogs are hoisted to repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME): web/src/i18n -> ../../../locales.
13	const LOCALES_DIR = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "..", "locales");
14	const SRC_DIR = join(import.meta.dirname, "..");
15	
16	function flatten(obj: Record<string, unknown>, prefix = ""): string[] {
17	  const keys: string[] = [];
18	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(obj)) {
19	    const key = prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k;
20	    if (v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v)) keys.push(...flatten(v as Record<string, unknown>, key));
21	    else keys.push(key);
22	  }
23	  return keys.sort();
24	}
25	
26	const loadKeys = (locale: string): string[] =>
27	  flatten(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES_DIR, locale, "common.json"), "utf8")) as Record<string, unknown>);
28	
29	describe("i18n key-parity — every locale has EXACTLY the en keys", () => {
30	  const enKeys = loadKeys("en");
31	  const others = readdirSync(LOCALES_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })
32	    .filter((d) => d.isDirectory() && d.name !== "en")
33	    .map((d) => d.name);
34	
35	  it("en is the non-empty source catalog", () => {
36	    expect(enKeys.length).toBeGreaterThan(20);
37	  });
38	
39	  for (const loc of others) {
40	    it(`${loc} matches en exactly (no missing, no extra keys)`, () => {
41	      const keys = loadKeys(loc);
42	      const missing = enKeys.filter((k) => !keys.includes(k));
43	      const extra = keys.filter((k) => !enKeys.includes(k));
44	      expect({ locale: loc, missing, extra }).toEqual({ locale: loc, missing: [], extra: [] });
45	    });
46	  }
47	});
48	
49	// Key-parity proves every locale has the same KEYS; this proves every locale's VALUE keeps the same
50	// simple `{placeholder}` set as en — so a translation can never silently drop a {count}/{error}/{min}/
51	// {filename}/{n}/… and break interpolation. (ICU plural headers like `{count, plural, …}` carry no
52	// simple {word} placeholder and match as empty on both sides, so they're correctly ignored.)
53	describe("placeholder parity — every locale preserves en's {placeholders}", () => {
54	  function flatEntries(obj: Record<string, unknown>, prefix = ""): Record<string, string> {
55	    const out: Record<string, string> = {};
56	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(obj)) {
57	      const key = prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k;
58	      if (v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v)) Object.assign(out, flatEntries(v as Record<string, unknown>, key));
59	      else out[key] = String(v);
60	    }
61	    return out;
62	  }
63	  const placeholders = (s: string) => (s.match(/\{(\w+)\}/g) ?? []).sort();
64	  const load = (loc: string) => flatEntries(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES_DIR, loc, "common.json"), "utf8")));
65	  const enE = load("en");
66	  const others = readdirSync(LOCALES_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })
67	    .filter((d) => d.isDirectory() && d.name !== "en")
68	    .map((d) => d.name);
69	
70	  for (const loc of others) {
71	    it(`${loc} preserves every {placeholder} that en uses`, () => {
72	      const locE = load(loc);
73	      const mismatches: Array<{ key: string; en: string[]; got: string[] }> = [];
74	      for (const key of Object.keys(enE)) {
75	        if (!(key in locE)) continue; // missing keys are the key-parity test's job
76	        const a = placeholders(enE[key]);
77	        const b = placeholders(locE[key]);
78	        if (a.join(",") !== b.join(",")) mismatches.push({ key, en: a, got: b });
79	      }
80	      expect(mismatches).toEqual([]);
81	    });
82	  }
83	});
84	
85	describe("es 3-form plural (CLDR one/many/other) — MULTILINGUAL Phase A", () => {
86	  // Spanish cardinal is {one, many, other}; every es ICU plural message MUST carry a `many {` arm
87	  // (the `many` category fires on large/compact numbers) or intl-messageformat silently falls back.
88	  // These are the plural-bearing keys in the catalog.
89	  const es = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES_DIR, "es", "common.json"), "utf8")) as Record<string, Record<string, string>>;
90	  const pluralKeys: Array<[string, string]> = [
91	    ["footer", "count"],
92	    ["footer", "floorSomeLead"],
93	    ["selection", "count"],
94	  ];
95	  for (const [ns, key] of pluralKeys) {
96	    it(`es ${ns}.${key} carries one/many/other arms`, () => {
97	      const msg = es[ns][key];
98	      expect(msg, `${ns}.${key}`).toMatch(/\bone\s*\{/);
99	      expect(msg, `${ns}.${key}`).toMatch(/\bmany\s*\{/);
100	      expect(msg, `${ns}.${key}`).toMatch(/\bother\s*\{/);
101	    });
102	  }
103	});
104	
105	describe("no-bare-strings — user-facing component attributes go through t()", () => {
106	  const FILES = [
107	    "App.tsx",
108	    "components/QueryBar.tsx",
109	    "components/TapFilters.tsx",
110	    "components/RallyGrid.tsx",
111	    "components/SelectionFooter.tsx",
112	    "components/CompileResult.tsx",
113	    "components/LanguageSwitcher.tsx",
114	    "components/IngestPanel.tsx",
115	  ];
116	  // A hardcoded placeholder / aria-label / title with letters is a bare-string regression — it
117	  // must be an expression ({t(...)}), not a quoted literal.
118	  const BARE_ATTR = /\b(?:placeholder|aria-label|title)\s*=\s*"[^"]*[A-Za-z]{2}[^"]*"/g;
119	
120	  for (const f of FILES) {
121	    it(`${f}: no hardcoded placeholder/aria-label/title`, () => {
122	      const src = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, f), "utf8");
123	      expect(src.match(BARE_ATTR) ?? []).toEqual([]);
124	    });
125	  }
126	});
127	
128	describe("Indic fonts (B4) — self-hosted OFL Noto, no runtime CDN", () => {
129	  it("main.tsx bundles the OFL Noto Kannada + Devanagari fonts (self-hosted, not Google Fonts)", () => {
130	    const main = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, "main.tsx"), "utf8");
131	    expect(main).toMatch(/@fontsource-variable\/noto-sans-kannada/);
132	    expect(main).toMatch(/@fontsource-variable\/noto-sans-devanagari/);
133	  });
134	  it("the font stack falls back to Noto for Kannada + Devanagari glyphs", () => {
135	    const css = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, "index.css"), "utf8");
136	    expect(css).toMatch(/Noto Sans Kannada Variable/);
137	    expect(css).toMatch(/Noto Sans Devanagari Variable/);
138	  });
139	});
140	
141	// The Studio SPA must render correctly on an AIR-GAPPED appliance (GPU/CPU/RAM, no internet) —
142	// every asset (fonts, styles, scripts) is bundled local, nothing pulled from a CDN/Google Fonts.
143	describe("offline / self-contained — no external runtime assets", () => {
144	  const CDN = /googleapis|gstatic|fonts\.google|cdnjs|unpkg|jsdelivr|\bcdn\.[a-z]/i;
145	  const FILES = [
146	    "../index.html", // web/index.html
147	    "main.tsx",
148	    "index.css",
149	  ];
150	  for (const rel of FILES) {
151	    it(`${rel}: no external font/CDN reference`, () => {
152	      expect(readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, rel), "utf8")).not.toMatch(CDN);
153	    });
154	  }
155	  it("web/index.html loads no absolute-URL <link>/<script src> (everything is bundled/local)", () => {
156	    const html = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, "..", "index.html"), "utf8");
157	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/(?:href|src)\s*=\s*["']https?:\/\//i);
158	  });
159	});

1	// @vitest-environment jsdom
2	//
3	// errorText is the single mapping from a classified error → user-facing text, shared by the ingest
4	// and build flows (A5). These pin: the engine message passes through verbatim, a detail-less engine
5	// error degrades to a friendly localized line (never a raw key), and every non-engine code resolves
6	// to a real catalog string in the shared `errors.*` namespace.
7	import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll } from "vitest";
8	import i18n from "../i18n";
9	import { errorText } from "./errorText";
10	import type { IngestErrorCode } from "./errors";
11	
12	beforeAll(async () => {
13	  await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
14	});
15	
16	describe("errorText", () => {
17	  it("an engine error shows the engine's own detail verbatim (most specific message)", () => {
18	    expect(errorText(i18n.t, { code: "engine", detail: "min_shot_count too low" })).toBe("min_shot_count too low");
19	  });
20	
21	  it("an engine error with no detail degrades to a friendly localized line (not a raw key)", () => {
22	    const s = errorText(i18n.t, { code: "engine" });
23	    expect(s).toBe(i18n.t("errors.engineGeneric"));
24	    expect(s).not.toMatch(/^errors\./);
25	    expect(s.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
26	  });
27	
28	  it("networkError maps to the friendly 'is it running?' line", () => {
29	    expect(errorText(i18n.t, { code: "networkError" })).toMatch(/is it running/i);
30	  });
31	
32	  it("every non-engine code resolves to a real catalog string (never a raw key)", () => {
33	    for (const code of ["invalidUri", "networkError", "emptyResult", "timeout"] as IngestErrorCode[]) {
34	      const s = errorText(i18n.t, { code });
35	      expect(s, code).not.toMatch(/^errors\./);
36	      expect(s.length, code).toBeGreaterThan(0);
37	    }
38	  });
39	
40	  it("localizes per the active locale (Kannada network message)", async () => {

1	// DRIFT GUARD for the one build step the otherwise no-build site has (tracker B3 / D-CATALOG-HOME):
2	// the catalogs the marketing site ships (site/public/locales/) MUST be byte-identical to the single
3	// source of truth (repo-root /locales/). If someone edits /locales without re-running the copy, this
4	// fails CI with the exact fix. Run `npm run copy:locales` to resync.
5	import { test } from "node:test";
6	import assert from "node:assert/strict";
7	import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
8	import { join } from "node:path";
9	
10	const SRC = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "locales");
11	const PUB = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "public", "locales");
12	
13	test("site/public/locales mirrors repo-root /locales exactly (else: npm run copy:locales)", () => {
14	  const locales = readdirSync(SRC, { withFileTypes: true }).filter((d) => d.isDirectory()).map((d) => d.name);
15	  assert.ok(locales.length >= 3, "expected at least en/kn/hi catalogs in /locales");
16	
17	  const shipped = new Set(readdirSync(PUB, { withFileTypes: true }).filter((d) => d.isDirectory()).map((d) => d.name));
18	  for (const loc of locales) {
19	    const src = readFileSync(join(SRC, loc, "common.json"), "utf8");
20	    assert.ok(shipped.has(loc), `site/public/locales/${loc} is missing — run npm run copy:locales`);
21	    const pub = readFileSync(join(PUB, loc, "common.json"), "utf8");
22	    assert.equal(pub, src, `site/public/locales/${loc}/common.json drifted from /locales/${loc} — run npm run copy:locales`);
23	  }
24	  // No stale extra locales shipped that aren't in the source of truth.
25	  for (const loc of shipped) {
26	    assert.ok(locales.includes(loc), `site/public/locales/${loc} has no source in /locales — run npm run copy:locales`);
27	  }
28	});

=== copy-locales script ===
// Mirror the single-source-of-truth catalogs (repo-root /locales, D-CATALOG-HOME) into the site's
// static public/ dir, so the dependency-free marketing site can fetch them at runtime via the
// t()/data-i18n shim (public/i18n.js). This copy is the ONLY build step the otherwise no-build site
// has. Run on demand (`npm run copy:locales`) or automatically before deploy (`predeploy`).
//
// The committed copy under site/public/locales/ is what actually ships (static hosts serve it with
// no build); the drift-guard test (src/locales-drift.test.js) fails CI if it goes stale vs /locales.
import { cpSync, rmSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const SRC = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "locales");
const DEST = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "public", "locales");

rmSync(DEST, { recursive: true, force: true });
mkdirSync(DEST, { recursive: true });
cpSync(SRC, DEST, { recursive: true });

console.log(`copy-locales: ${readdirSync(DEST).join(", ")} -> site/public/locales/`);

## Summary

**[khelsutra#77](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/77)** — squash-merged to `master` (`9f1ec9c`) on green CI (web ✓, site ✓, Cloudflare ✓). Branch & worktree cleaned up.

### What I fixed (every audit finding)

**Correctness**
- **Brand stays Latin `KhelSutra`** — dropped `खेलसूत्र` (hi) and `ಖೇಲ್‌ಸೂತ್ರ` (kn) in `ingest.intro/reassure/fileHint`. I extended this to the Kannada console too (not just hi), since en and the A3-reviewed `site.*` use Latin everywhere and the `errors.networkError` fix required a brand reference anyway.
- **`footer.floorSomeRest`** (kn+hi) — removed the leaked `min_shot_count` token (+ kn's `<code>` tag) and "engine" → kn `(ಅವು ತುಂಬಾ ಚಿಕ್ಕದಾಗಿವೆ).`, hi `(वे बहुत छोटी हैं)।`.

**Jargon leak** — `footer.floorOne/floorAll` rephrased passively (no "engine"); `errors.networkError` → brand; `ingest.intro/reassure` de-claused to match en. I also aligned `ingest.hint` and `errors.invalidUri` (same leak class — en says "machine running KhelSutra", not "engine"). **Left `ingest.uriAriaLabel` as-is** because en itself uses "engine host"/"bucket URL" there.

**Hindi fluency** — `app.badge`, `app.intro`, `grid.shots/shotsTitle` (`आज़माइशी`/`अभी बन रही है`), `grid.shotsConfidence` (`कितना पक्का`), `query.support`, `result.dismiss` (`बंद करें`).

**Kannada** — `bulk.invert` → `ಆಯ್ಕೆ ಬದಲಿಸಿ`; `result.dismiss` → `ಮುಚ್ಚಿ`.

### Verification (all run locally, all green)
- Every `<b>/<i>/<x>` and `{min}/{count}/{conf}` placeholder preserved; key-parity en/kn/hi = 154 each.
- `web`: typecheck clean + **177/177** (hygiene key-parity, placeholder-parity, es 3-form, the 17 i18n render tests). Updated the one test that pins kn `networkError`.
- `site`: **69/69**, incl. the drift guard — I re-ran `copy:locales` so `site/public/locales` stays byte-identical.
- Rebased onto latest `origin/master` mid-task (master churned: A6 #75, A7 #76 landed) — clean, no conflicts.

### Notes
- **5 files** changed: `locales/{kn,hi}/common.json`, `site/public/locales/{kn,hi}/common.json`, `web/src/lib/errorText.test.ts`.
- Catalogs remain tagged (in the commit/PR body): **AI-draft + adversarial-review pass; native review still pending** — I couldn't put this tag inside the JSON without breaking key-parity, so it lives in the commit message, PR body, and the session handoff (which I updated to mark this task done).
- Where the audit gave example keys, I applied each bug *class* consistently across the named namespaces; subjective fluency rewrites only where the per-language audit flagged them.

### 114. user (2026-06-21T19:18:35.028Z)

1	# Live-host parity — the site runs on TWO hosts (Cloudflare Worker + the DO droplet's
2	# Node/SQLite twin) that must serve byte-identical pages. The unit suite proves the code
3	# works on each host; it CANNOT see that the two LIVE URLs have drifted (e.g. a merge
4	# auto-deployed Cloudflare but the droplet wasn't re-synced from git). This runs the
5	# existing scripts/parity-check.sh against both live URLs on a schedule — NOT on PRs, so
6	# it never blocks development; a failure surfaces real production drift to the owner.
7	name: live-parity
8	
9	on:
10	  schedule:
11	    - cron: "17 6 * * *" # daily ~06:17 UTC
12	  workflow_dispatch: {} # and on demand
13	
14	# Avoid overlapping runs if a manual dispatch races the cron.
15	concurrency:
16	  group: live-parity
17	  cancel-in-progress: false
18	
19	jobs:
20	  parity:
21	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
22	    steps:
23	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
24	      - name: Compare the two live hosts byte-for-byte
25	        env:
26	          CF_URL: https://khelsutra.guru
27	          VM_URL: http://168.144.126.176:8787
28	        run: bash site/scripts/parity-check.sh
29

1	// i18n scaffold (tracker B1) — the shared localization foundation for KhelSutra Studio.
2	//
3	// Per docs/FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md + the engine's I18N_PLAN.md:
4	//   - keys, never prose (t("app.title"), enforced later by a no-bare-strings lint, B2);
5	//   - ICU MessageFormat for interpolation/plurals (Kannada/Hindi plural rules live in the catalog,
6	//     not the code — replaces the SPA's hand-rolled `count > 1 ? "s" : ""`);
7	//   - one negotiation order shared with the marketing site (B3);
8	//   - always-fallback to `en` so a missing translation degrades to English, never a raw key.
9	import i18n from "i18next";
10	import { initReactI18next } from "react-i18next";
11	import LanguageDetector from "i18next-browser-languagedetector";
12	import ICU from "i18next-icu";
13	// Catalogs are HOISTED to repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME) — the single source of truth shared
14	// by this SPA and the marketing site's t() shim (tracker B3). Vite resolves the workspace root via
15	// the repo `.git` marker, so importing the parent-dir /locales works in dev, build, and vitest.
16	import enCommon from "../../../locales/en/common.json";
17	import knCommon from "../../../locales/kn/common.json";
18	import hiCommon from "../../../locales/hi/common.json";
19	import esCommon from "../../../locales/es/common.json";
20	import daCommon from "../../../locales/da/common.json";
21	import idCommon from "../../../locales/id/common.json";
22	
23	// Supported locales: en/kn/hi foundation (FRIENDLY D6) + MULTILINGUAL_EXPANSION Phase A (es/da/id,
24	// Latin, catalog-only). Anything else falls back to `en`.
25	export const SUPPORTED_LOCALES = ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"] as const;
26	export type Locale = (typeof SUPPORTED_LOCALES)[number];
27	export const DEFAULT_LOCALE: Locale = "en";
28	
29	// ONE persisted-preference key, shared with the marketing site's t() shim (D9 / tracker B3), so a
30	// visitor's language choice carries across both surfaces.
31	export const LANG_STORAGE_KEY = "khelsutra.lang";
32	
33	// Catalogs live in repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME), consumed by both surfaces. `kn` + `hi`
34	// shipped (B5/B6, AI-drafted → native review pending). A missing key in any locale still falls back
35	// to `en` — the by-design invariant.
36	const resources = {
37	  en: { common: enCommon },
38	  kn: { common: knCommon },
39	  hi: { common: hiCommon },
40	  es: { common: esCommon },
41	  da: { common: daCommon },
42	  id: { common: idCommon },
43	} as const;
44	
45	void i18n
46	  .use(ICU)
47	  .use(LanguageDetector)
48	  .use(initReactI18next)
49	  .init({
50	    resources,
51	    fallbackLng: DEFAULT_LOCALE,
52	    supportedLngs: [...SUPPORTED_LOCALES],
53	    nonExplicitSupportedLngs: true, // en-IN -> en
54	    defaultNS: "common",
55	    ns: ["common"],
56	    interpolation: { escapeValue: false }, // React already escapes
57	    returnNull: false,
58	    // Negotiation order (I18N_PLAN §2.3): persisted pref -> ?lang= -> browser -> en.
59	    detection: {
60	      order: ["localStorage", "querystring", "navigator"],
61	      lookupLocalStorage: LANG_STORAGE_KEY,
62	      lookupQuerystring: "lang",
63	      caches: ["localStorage"],
64	    },
65	    react: { useSuspense: false },
66	  });
67	
68	// Keep <html lang> in sync with the active locale (screen readers + SEO use it). Reflects the
69	// resolved content language, so an `en` fallback for an absent locale still reads as `en`.
70	function syncDocumentLang(lng: string) {
71	  if (typeof document !== "undefined") {
72	    document.documentElement.lang = (i18n.resolvedLanguage || lng || DEFAULT_LOCALE).split("-")[0];
73	  }
74	}
75	i18n.on("languageChanged", syncDocumentLang);
76	syncDocumentLang(i18n.language ?? DEFAULT_LOCALE);
77	
78	export default i18n;
79

1	// Sticky compile footer (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md P4): live selection summary, a reel-name input,
2	// the Build button, and — above all — an HONEST min_shot_count warning. The engine silently drops
3	// selected rallies below stitching.min_shot_count; we surface that BEFORE Build so a rally never
4	// vanishes without a reason (D7). We warn, we do NOT override the floor (D11).
5	import { useTranslation, Trans } from "react-i18next";
6	import { mmss } from "../lib/format";
7	import type { FloorRisk } from "../lib/floor";
8	
9	interface Props {
10	  count: number;
11	  totalSec: number;
12	  reelName: string;
13	  onReelNameChange: (v: string) => void;
14	  onBuild: () => void;
15	  /** A compile is in flight (Build wired in P5). */
16	  building?: boolean;
17	  /** Non-null when some selected rallies sit below the engine floor. */
18	  warning: FloorRisk | null;
19	}
20	
21	export function SelectionFooter({ count, totalSec, reelName, onReelNameChange, onBuild, building, warning }: Props) {
22	  const { t } = useTranslation();
23	  const buildDisabled = count === 0 || building || warning?.allDropped === true;
24	
25	  return (
26	    <footer className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 border-t border-black/10 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur">
27	      {warning && (
28	        <div
29	          role="status"
30	          data-testid="floor-warning"
31	          className={
32	            "border-b px-6 py-2 text-sm " +
33	            (warning.allDropped
34	              ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-800"
35	              : "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-900")
36	          }
37	        >
38	          {warning.allDropped ? (
39	            count === 1 ? (
40	              <Trans i18nKey="footer.floorOne" values={{ min: warning.minShotCount }} components={{ b: <strong /> }} />
41	            ) : (
42	              <Trans i18nKey="footer.floorAll" values={{ count, min: warning.minShotCount }} components={{ b: <strong /> }} />
43	            )
44	          ) : (
45	            <>
46	              <strong>{t("footer.floorSomeLead", { count: warning.atRisk.length })}</strong>{" "}
47	              <Trans i18nKey="footer.floorSomeRest" values={{ min: warning.minShotCount }} components={{ code: <code /> }} />
48	            </>
49	          )}
50	        </div>
51	      )}
52	
53	      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl flex-wrap items-center gap-3 px-6 py-3">
54	        <span className="text-sm text-ink/80" data-testid="selection-summary">
55	          <strong className="text-ink">{t("footer.count", { count })}</strong> {t("footer.summaryRest", { total: mmss(totalSec) })}
56	        </span>
57	
58	        <label className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-ink/60">
59	          <span className="hidden sm:inline">{t("footer.reelName")}</span>
60	          <input
61	            type="text"
62	            value={reelName}
63	            onChange={(e) => onReelNameChange(e.target.value)}
64	            placeholder={t("footer.reelNamePlaceholder")}
65	            aria-label={t("footer.reelName")}
66	            className="min-h-11 w-40 rounded-lg border border-black/15 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-ink outline-none focus:border-green"
67	          />
68	        </label>
69	
70	        <button
71	          type="button"
72	          onClick={onBuild}
73	          disabled={buildDisabled}
74	          className="min-h-11 rounded-full bg-green px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#04140D] disabled:opacity-40"
75	        >
76	          {building ? t("footer.building") : t("footer.build", { count })}
77	        </button>
78	      </div>
79	    </footer>
80	  );
81	}

1	// The ONE place a classified error (lib/errors.ts) becomes user-facing text. Both surfaces use it:
2	// the ingest flow (IngestPanel) and the build flow (App.onBuild → CompileResult), so a 5xx / engine-
3	// down failure reads the same friendly way everywhere instead of a raw "HTTP 500" / fetch message.
4	//
5	// `engine` carries the engine's OWN message (the most specific) → shown verbatim, never translated.
6	// Every other code maps to a friendly localized line in the shared `errors.*` namespace. Kept out of
7	// errors.ts so that module stays i18n-free and unit-testable without a translation context.
8	import type { TFunction } from "i18next";
9	import type { IngestError } from "./errors";
10	
11	export function errorText(t: TFunction, e: IngestError): string {
12	  if (e.code === "engine") return e.detail ?? t("errors.engineGeneric");
13	  return t(`errors.${e.code}`);
14	}

### 127. user (2026-06-21T19:21:45.096Z)

1	// The stitching.min_shot_count floor — surfaced, never hidden (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md D7/D11).
2	// /api/compile silently drops selected rallies below the floor; the UI WARNS before Build so a
3	// rally never vanishes without explanation. An override is deferred (owner gate, Launch Report).
4	import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";
5	import type { Selection } from "./selection";
6	import { selectedRallies } from "./selection";
7	
8	export interface FloorRisk {
9	  /** The configured stitching.min_shot_count the engine enforces. */
10	  minShotCount: number;
11	  /** Selected rallies the engine WILL drop at compile (numeric shot_count below the floor). */
12	  atRisk: RallySegment[];
13	  /** True when EVERY selected rally is below the floor → /api/compile returns 400 (build fails). */
14	  allDropped: boolean;
15	}
16	
17	/** Which SELECTED rallies the engine's min_shot_count floor would drop at compile.
18	 * Mirrors backend/main.py:362-383 EXACTLY: a rally is dropped iff it has a NUMERIC shot_count
19	 * strictly below the floor; a rally WITHOUT a shot_count is EXEMPT (kept). Returns null when there
20	 * is nothing to warn about (no floor configured, empty selection, or none at risk). */
21	export function floorRisk(
22	  rallies: RallySegment[],
23	  selection: Selection,
24	  minShotCount: number | undefined,
25	): FloorRisk | null {
26	  if (minShotCount == null) return null;
27	  const selected = selectedRallies(rallies, selection);
28	  if (selected.length === 0) return null;
29	  const atRisk = selected.filter((r) => {
30	    const sc = r.metadata?.shot_count;
31	    return typeof sc === "number" && sc < minShotCount; // exempt when shot_count is absent
32	  });
33	  if (atRisk.length === 0) return null;
34	  return { minShotCount, atRisk, allDropped: atRisk.length === selected.length };
35	}

1	// Vitest setup (runs before each test file's imports). DOM-env only: jsdom under Node 26 does not
2	// expose a Web Storage `localStorage`, which the i18n language detector and the persistence test
3	// both need. Node-env test files (typeof window === "undefined") skip this entirely.
4	if (typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof globalThis.localStorage === "undefined") {
5	  let store: Record<string, string> = {};
6	  const mem: Storage = {
7	    get length() {
8	      return Object.keys(store).length;
9	    },
10	    clear() {
11	      store = {};
12	    },
13	    getItem(key: string) {
14	      return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(store, key) ? store[key] : null;
15	    },
16	    key(index: number) {
17	      return Object.keys(store)[index] ?? null;
18	    },
19	    removeItem(key: string) {
20	      delete store[key];
21	    },
22	    setItem(key: string, value: string) {
23	      store[key] = String(value);
24	    },
25	  };
26	  Object.defineProperty(window, "localStorage", { value: mem, configurable: true, writable: true });
27	  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, "localStorage", { value: mem, configurable: true, writable: true });
28	}

### 145. assistant (2026-06-21T19:26:26.647Z)

// @vitest-environment jsdom
//
// Coverage + REGRESSION guard for SelectionFooter's floor-warning block (previously uncovered) AND
// the kn/hi native-review fixes (#77). The warning renders footer.floorOne / floorAll /
// floorSomeLead+floorSomeRest — the very keys the adversarial audit cleaned of the leaked
// `min_shot_count` token and the "engine"/"server" jargon. Rendering the REAL catalog in en/kn/hi and
// asserting the cleaned copy means a regression that re-introduces the token/jargon fails right here
// (not just in the data-level catalog-invariants test) — i.e. the bug can't reach the screen.
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from "@testing-library/react";
import i18n from "../i18n";
import { SelectionFooter } from "./SelectionFooter";
import type { FloorRisk } from "../lib/floor";
import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";

// Only the array LENGTH matters to the footer (floorSomeLead shows atRisk.length); content is unused.
const risks = (n: number): RallySegment[] => Array.from({ length: n }, () => ({}) as RallySegment);
const baseProps = { totalSec: 90, reelName: "", onReelNameChange: () => {}, onBuild: () => {} };
const warnText = () => screen.getByTestId("floor-warning").textContent ?? "";
const buildBtn = () => screen.getByRole("button") as HTMLButtonElement; // the footer has exactly one button

afterEach(cleanup);

describe("SelectionFooter — floor warning renders the audit-clean copy (en)", () => {
  beforeEach(async () => {
    window.localStorage.clear();
    await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
  });

  it("allDropped + a single selection → floorOne, passive phrasing (no 'engine'), Build blocked", () => {
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={1} warning={{ minShotCount: 6, atRisk: risks(1), allDropped: true }} />);
    expect(warnText()).toContain("6"); // {min}
    expect(warnText()).toMatch(/skipped/i);
    expect(warnText()).not.toMatch(/engine/i); // audit: en is engine-free, so kn/hi/en stay engine-free
    expect(buildBtn().disabled).toBe(true); // every selected rally below the floor → build would fail
  });

  it("allDropped + many → floorAll (count + min), Build blocked", () => {
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={3} warning={{ minShotCount: 6, atRisk: risks(3), allDropped: true }} />);
    expect(warnText()).toContain("3"); // {count}
    expect(warnText()).toContain("6"); // {min}
    expect(warnText()).not.toMatch(/engine/i);
    expect(buildBtn().disabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it("partial risk → floorSomeLead + floorSomeRest, NO leaked min_shot_count token, Build allowed", () => {
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={5} warning={{ minShotCount: 6, atRisk: risks(2), allDropped: false }} />);
    const t = warnText();
    expect(t).toContain("2"); // floorSomeLead count = atRisk.length
    expect(t).toContain("6"); // {min}
    expect(t).not.toMatch(/min_shot_count/); // audit: the internal code token must never surface
    expect(t).not.toMatch(/engine/i);
    expect(buildBtn().disabled).toBe(false); // only SOME are at risk → build can proceed
  });
});

describe("SelectionFooter — floor warning is jargon-free in kn + hi (#77 regression)", () => {
  beforeEach(() => window.localStorage.clear());
  afterEach(async () => {
    await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
  });

  it("kn: floorOne + floorSome carry no 'ಎಂಜಿನ್' and no leaked token", async () => {
    await i18n.changeLanguage("kn");
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={1} warning={{ minShotCount: 6, atRisk: risks(1), allDropped: true }} />);
    expect(warnText()).not.toContain("ಎಂಜಿನ್");
    cleanup();
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={5} warning={{ minShotCount: 6, atRisk: risks(2), allDropped: false }} />);
    expect(warnText()).not.toContain("ಎಂಜಿನ್");
    expect(warnText()).not.toMatch(/min_shot_count/);
  });

  it("hi: floorOne + floorSome carry no 'इंजन' and no leaked token", async () => {
    await i18n.changeLanguage("hi");
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={1} warning={{ minShotCount: 6, atRisk: risks(1), allDropped: true }} />);
    expect(warnText()).not.toContain("इंजन");
    cleanup();
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={5} warning={{ minShotCount: 6, atRisk: risks(2), allDropped: false }} />);
    expect(warnText()).not.toContain("इंजन");
    expect(warnText()).not.toMatch(/min_shot_count/);
  });
});

describe("SelectionFooter — no-warning controls (summary, reel name, build button)", () => {
  beforeEach(async () => {
    window.localStorage.clear();
    await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
  });

  it("no warning → no floor-warning region; summary + enabled Build render", () => {
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={4} warning={null} />);
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("floor-warning")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByTestId("selection-summary").textContent).toMatch(/4 rallies/);
    expect(buildBtn().disabled).toBe(false);
  });

  it("building → the button shows the 'Building…' label and is disabled", () => {
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={2} warning={null} building />);
    expect(buildBtn().textContent).toMatch(/Building/);
    expect(buildBtn().disabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it("empty selection → Build disabled", () => {
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={0} warning={null} />);
    expect(buildBtn().disabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it("editing the reel name and clicking Build fire their callbacks", () => {
    const onReelNameChange = vi.fn();
    const onBuild = vi.fn();
    render(<SelectionFooter {...baseProps} count={3} warning={null} onReelNameChange={onReelNameChange} onBuild={onBuild} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Reel name"), { target: { value: "finals" } });
    expect(onReelNameChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith("finals");
    fireEvent.click(buildBtn());
    expect(onBuild).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

### 147. assistant (2026-06-21T19:26:50.573Z)

// Content regression guards for the localization catalogs — the rules the adversarial translation
// audit (#77) established, pinned so the specific bugs can never silently return. This complements
// hygiene.test.ts (which proves key/placeholder PARITY) by asserting catalog CONTENT:
//   1. no internal code-token / <code> leaks in any locale,
//   2. the brand "KhelSutra" is never transliterated into an Indic script,
//   3. engine/server/bucket jargon appears ONLY on keys where the en source uses it,
//   4. the specific flagged mistranslations stay fixed.
// These are pure data assertions over repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME) — no render context needed.
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const LOCALES_DIR = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "..", "locales");

type Flat = Record<string, string>;
function flatEntries(obj: Record<string, unknown>, prefix = ""): Flat {
  const out: Flat = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(obj)) {
    const key = prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k;
    if (v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v)) Object.assign(out, flatEntries(v as Record<string, unknown>, key));
    else out[key] = String(v);
  }
  return out;
}
const load = (loc: string): Flat =>
  flatEntries(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES_DIR, loc, "common.json"), "utf8")) as Record<string, unknown>);

const ALL_LOCALES = readdirSync(LOCALES_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })
  .filter((d) => d.isDirectory())
  .map((d) => d.name);
const en = load("en");

describe("catalog content — no internal token / HTML-tag leaks (every locale)", () => {
  for (const loc of ALL_LOCALES) {
    it(`${loc}: no 'min_shot_count' code token or <code> tag in any user-facing string`, () => {
      const offenders = Object.entries(load(loc))
        .filter(([, v]) => /min_shot_count/.test(v) || /<\/?code>/i.test(v))
        .map(([k]) => k);
      expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
    });
  }
});

describe("catalog content — the brand is never transliterated (Indic locales)", () => {
  const TRANSLIT: Record<string, string> = { kn: "ಖೇಲ್‌ಸೂತ್ರ", hi: "खेलसूत्र" };
  for (const loc of ["kn", "hi"] as const) {
    it(`${loc}: no Indic transliteration of "KhelSutra" anywhere`, () => {
      const offenders = Object.entries(load(loc))
        .filter(([, v]) => v.includes(TRANSLIT[loc]))
        .map(([k]) => k);
      expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
    });
    it(`${loc}: the ingest console keys keep the Latin brand`, () => {
      const e = load(loc);
      for (const key of ["ingest.intro", "ingest.reassure", "ingest.fileHint"]) {
        expect(e[key], `${loc} ${key}`).toContain("KhelSutra");
      }
    });
  }
});

describe("catalog content — engine/server/bucket jargon only where en uses it (Indic locales)", () => {
  // en deliberately avoids these terms in user-facing body copy (it says "KhelSutra" / "cloud-storage"
  // / passive voice) — EXCEPT the technical uriAriaLabel ("engine host", "bucket URL"). So the rule is
  // self-maintaining: a localized jargon term is allowed ONLY on a key whose en value also uses the
  // English word. Reintroducing "engine"/"server"/"bucket" anywhere else fails here.
  const JARGON: Record<string, ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, string]>> = {
    kn: [
      ["ಎಂಜಿನ್", "engine"],
      ["ಸರ್ವರ್", "server"],
      ["ಬಕೆಟ್", "bucket"],
    ],
    hi: [
      ["इंजन", "engine"],
      ["सर्वर", "server"],
      ["बकेट", "bucket"],
    ],
  };
  for (const loc of ["kn", "hi"] as const) {
    it(`${loc}: a jargon term appears only on keys whose en value also uses it`, () => {
      const e = load(loc);
      const violations: Array<{ key: string; term: string; en: string }> = [];
      for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(e)) {
        for (const [term, eng] of JARGON[loc]) {
          if (val.includes(term) && !(en[key] ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(eng)) {
            violations.push({ key, term, en: en[key] ?? "(absent)" });
          }
        }
      }
      expect(violations).toEqual([]);
    });
  }
});

describe("catalog content — flagged mistranslations stay fixed (Kannada/Hindi word choice)", () => {
  it("kn bulk.invert is not the 'rotate' mistranslation (ತಿರುಗಿಸಿ)", () => {
    expect(load("kn")["bulk.invert"]).not.toBe("ತಿರುಗಿಸಿ");
  });
  it("kn result.dismiss is not the legalistic 'ವಜಾಗೊಳಿಸಿ'", () => {
    expect(load("kn")["result.dismiss"]).not.toBe("ವಜಾಗೊಳಿಸಿ");
  });
  it("hi result.dismiss is not the harsh 'खारिज करें'", () => {
    expect(load("hi")["result.dismiss"]).not.toBe("खारिज करें");
  });
});

### 179. user (2026-06-21T19:40:20.334Z)

1	// The fast-entry query bar (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md P3). A deterministic, OFFLINE parser turns
2	// plain text ("top 15 longest", "over 10s") into a selection over the SAME shared set the
3	// checkboxes use. It is honest by construction: a live "parsed-pill" shows exactly how the
4	// text was understood, and concepts with no data behind them (shot-type / player / score) are
5	// greyed out as "not available yet" — never silently faked.
6	import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
7	import type { FormEvent } from "react";
8	import { useTranslation, Trans } from "react-i18next";
9	import { parseQuery, summarize } from "../lib/query";
10	import type { ParsedQuery } from "../lib/query";
11	
12	interface Props {
13	  /** Apply a parsed query to the rally set (App runs runQuery + writes the shared selection).
14	   * `source` distinguishes a typed-and-submitted query from an example-chip tap (for the trace). */
15	  onApply: (q: ParsedQuery, source: "typed" | "example") => void;
16	}
17	
18	// Each example is a real, parseable query — tapping one fills the box AND applies it. Led by rally
19	// LENGTH (the trusted axis); shot-count filtering exists but is experimental, so it's not featured
20	// here (a test pins that every example parses to a recognized, fully-available query).
21	export const EXAMPLES = ["over 10s", "top 15 longest", "shortest first", "first 10"];
22	
23	// Parsed-pill chips: legible on a phone (text-sm, not text-xs) — A4.
24	const CHIP = "rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-sm font-medium";
25	
26	export function QueryBar({ onApply }: Props) {
27	  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
28	  // The example chips are ENGLISH query syntax (the parser is English-only). For a non-English user
29	  // they're an untappable wall — hide them and let the localized TapFilters (A9) own the tap path.
30	  const isEnglish = (i18n.resolvedLanguage ?? i18n.language ?? "en").split("-")[0] === "en";
31	  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
32	  const parsed = useMemo(() => parseQuery(value), [value]);
33	  const clauses = summarize(parsed);
34	  const typed = value.trim().length > 0;
35	
36	  const applyText = (text: string) => {
37	    setValue(text);
38	    onApply(parseQuery(text), "example");
39	  };
40	  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
41	    e.preventDefault();
42	    if (parsed.recognized) onApply(parsed, "typed");
43	  };
44	
45	  return (
46	    <div>
47	      <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
48	        <input
49	          type="text"
50	          value={value}
51	          onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
52	          placeholder={t("query.placeholder")}
53	          aria-label={t("query.ariaLabel")}
54	          className="min-h-11 min-w-[16rem] flex-1 rounded-xl border border-black/15 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:border-green"
55	        />
56	        <button
57	          type="submit"
58	          disabled={!parsed.recognized}
59	          className="min-h-11 rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-40"
60	        >
61	          {t("query.apply")}
62	        </button>
63	      </form>
64	
65	      {/* Live interpretation — transparent + hand-correctable (no hidden NL guessing). */}
66	      <div aria-live="polite" data-testid="query-pill" className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
67	        {clauses.map((c) => (
68	          <span key={c} className={CHIP + " bg-green/15 text-green"}>
69	            {c}
70	          </span>
71	        ))}
72	        {parsed.unavailable.map((u) => (
73	          <span
74	            key={u.token}
75	            title={t("query.notAvailableTitle", { token: u.token, kind: u.kind })}
76	            className={CHIP + " bg-black/5 text-ink/45"}
77	          >
78	            <span className="line-through">{u.token}</span> · {t("query.notAvailable")}
79	          </span>
80	        ))}
81	        {typed && !parsed.recognized && parsed.unavailable.length === 0 && (
82	          <span className="text-xs text-ink/50">{t("query.unreadable")}</span>
83	        )}
84	        {!typed && <span className="text-xs text-ink/50">{t("query.hint")}</span>}
85	      </div>
86	
87	      {/* Surface the "not available yet" reason as VISIBLE text (the per-chip title is hover-only,
88	          so it never appears on a phone) — A4. */}
89	      {parsed.unavailable.length > 0 && (
90	        <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-ink/50">{t("query.notAvailableHelp")}</p>
91	      )}
92	
93	      {isEnglish && (
94	        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
95	          <span className="text-ink/40">{t("query.try")}</span>
96	          {EXAMPLES.map((ex) => (
97	            <button
98	              key={ex}
99	              type="button"
100	              onClick={() => applyText(ex)}
101	              className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-full border border-black/15 px-4 text-sm hover:border-green"
102	            >
103	              {ex}
104	            </button>
105	          ))}
106	        </div>
107	      )}
108	
109	      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/40">
110	        <Trans i18nKey="query.support" components={{ b: <strong className="text-ink/60" />, x: <span className="text-amber-700" /> }} />
111	      </p>
112	    </div>
113	  );
114	}
115

### 185. assistant (2026-06-21T19:42:48.240Z)

// Content regression guards for the localization catalogs — the rules the adversarial translation
// audit (#77) established, pinned so the specific bugs can never silently return. This complements
// hygiene.test.ts (which proves key/placeholder PARITY) by asserting catalog CONTENT. Each block maps
// to a concrete #77 bug class; the mutation that reintroduces the bug fails here. Pure data assertions
// over repo-root /locales (D-CATALOG-HOME) — no render context needed.
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const LOCALES_DIR = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "..", "..", "locales");

type Flat = Record<string, string>;
function flatEntries(obj: Record<string, unknown>, prefix = ""): Flat {
  const out: Flat = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(obj)) {
    const key = prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k;
    if (v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v)) Object.assign(out, flatEntries(v as Record<string, unknown>, key));
    else out[key] = String(v);
  }
  return out;
}
const load = (loc: string): Flat =>
  flatEntries(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES_DIR, loc, "common.json"), "utf8")) as Record<string, unknown>);

const ALL_LOCALES = readdirSync(LOCALES_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })
  .filter((d) => d.isDirectory())
  .map((d) => d.name);
const en = load("en");

// (1) Internal-token / debug-tag leaks — EVERY locale. The engine's internal `min_shot_count` config
// name leaked into footer.floorSomeRest (#77, kn even wrapped it in <code>). No developer/markup tag
// (code/kbd/samp/tt/var) or that token may reach any user-facing string.
describe("catalog content — no internal token / debug-tag leaks (every locale)", () => {
  const TOKEN = /min_shot_count/;
  const DEBUG_TAG = /<\/?(?:code|kbd|samp|tt|var)\b/i;
  for (const loc of ALL_LOCALES) {
    it(`${loc}: no 'min_shot_count' token or <code>/<kbd>/<samp>/<tt>/<var> tag`, () => {
      const offenders = Object.entries(load(loc))
        .filter(([, v]) => TOKEN.test(v) || DEBUG_TAG.test(v))
        .map(([k]) => k);
      expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
    });
  }
});

// (2) Brand — never transliterated, and present (Latin) on EVERY key en uses it on. The positive rule
// is the real guard: self-maintaining (auto-covers future brand keys, incl. errors.* / ingest.hint that
// the old 3-key denylist missed) and it catches ANY transliteration spelling at once — a transliterated
// value simply won't contain Latin "KhelSutra". The Indic-spelling denylist is cheap defence-in-depth.
describe("catalog content — the brand stays Latin 'KhelSutra' everywhere en uses it", () => {
  const brandKeys = Object.keys(en).filter((k) => en[k].includes("KhelSutra"));

  it("en actually carries the brand on several keys (so the guard is meaningful)", () => {
    expect(brandKeys.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(6);
  });

  for (const loc of ["kn", "hi"] as const) {
    it(`${loc}: Latin "KhelSutra" is present on every key whose en value uses it`, () => {
      const e = load(loc);
      const missing = brandKeys.filter((k) => !(e[k] ?? "").includes("KhelSutra"));
      expect(missing).toEqual([]);
    });
  }

  const TRANSLIT: Record<string, string> = { kn: "ಖೇಲ್‌ಸೂತ್ರ", hi: "खेलसूत्र" };
  for (const loc of ["kn", "hi"] as const) {
    it(`${loc}: the known Indic transliteration of the brand never appears`, () => {
      const offenders = Object.entries(load(loc))
        .filter(([, v]) => v.includes(TRANSLIT[loc]))
        .map(([k]) => k);
      expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
    });
  }
});

// (3) Engine/server/bucket/backend jargon only where en uses it (kn/hi). Self-maintaining: a localized
// jargon term — the Latin word OR an Indic transliteration — is allowed ONLY on a key whose en value
// also uses the English word. en avoids these in body copy (uses the brand / "cloud-storage" / passive
// voice) EXCEPT the technical ingest.uriAriaLabel + uriPlaceholder; #77 leaked them into kn/hi elsewhere.
describe("catalog content — engine/server/bucket jargon only where en uses it (kn/hi)", () => {
  const ENGLISH = ["engine", "server", "bucket", "backend"] as const;
  const TRANSLIT: Record<string, Record<(typeof ENGLISH)[number], string[]>> = {
    kn: { engine: ["ಎಂಜಿನ್"], server: ["ಸರ್ವರ್", "ಪರಿಚಾರಕ"], bucket: ["ಬಕೆಟ್"], backend: ["ಬ್ಯಾಕೆಂಡ್"] },
    hi: { engine: ["इंजन"], server: ["सर्वर", "परिचारक"], bucket: ["बकेट"], backend: ["बैकएंड"] },
  };
  for (const loc of ["kn", "hi"] as const) {
    it(`${loc}: a jargon term (Latin or transliterated) appears only where en uses the English word`, () => {
      const e = load(loc);
      const violations: Array<{ key: string; term: string; en: string }> = [];
      for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(e)) {
        const enUsesWord = (w: string) => new RegExp(`\\b${w}\\b`, "i").test(en[key] ?? "");
        for (const w of ENGLISH) {
          const present = [
            ...(new RegExp(`\\b${w}\\b`, "i").test(val) ? [w] : []),
            ...TRANSLIT[loc][w].filter((t) => val.includes(t)),
          ];
          for (const term of present) if (!enUsesWord(w)) violations.push({ key, term, en: en[key] ?? "(absent)" });
        }
      }
      expect(violations).toEqual([]);
    });
  }
});

// (4) Hindi de-Sanskritization (#77 class d) — the over-Sanskritized / stiff terms must stay replaced
// on the AUDITED keys. Key-scoped (not a repo-wide ban) so legitimate marketing prose elsewhere is not
// constrained, while still catching a silent revert of the exact bug.
describe("catalog content — Hindi de-Sanskritization stays fixed (#77 class d)", () => {
  const hi = load("hi");
  const BANNED: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ["grid.shots", "प्रयोगात्मक"], // "experimental" calque
    ["grid.shotsTitle", "प्रयोगात्मक"],
    ["grid.shotsTitle", "विकासाधीन"], // "under development" bureaucratese
    ["query.support", "प्रयोगात्मक"],
    ["query.support", "विकासाधीन"],
    ["grid.shotsConfidence", "विश्वास"], // "faith" — wrong sense for statistical confidence
    ["app.badge", "प्रति-रैली"], // "per-rally" calque
  ];
  for (const [key, term] of BANNED) {
    it(`hi ${key} no longer contains the stiff "${term}"`, () => {
      expect(hi).toHaveProperty(key);
      expect(hi[key] ?? "").not.toContain(term);
    });
  }
  it("hi keeps the friendlier replacements (आज़माइशी / कितना पक्का)", () => {
    expect(hi["grid.shots"]).toContain("आज़माइशी");
    expect(hi["grid.shotsTitle"]).toContain("आज़माइशी");
    expect(hi["query.support"]).toContain("आज़माइशी");
    expect(hi["grid.shotsConfidence"]).toContain("कितना पक्का");
  });
});

// (5) Flagged mistranslations — substring guard (.not.toContain) so a padded/wrapped variant of the
// banned term ("ಆಯ್ಕೆ ತಿರುಗಿಸಿ", a trailing space, …) is still caught.
describe("catalog content — flagged mistranslations stay fixed (kn/hi word choice)", () => {
  it("kn bulk.invert drops the 'rotate' mistranslation (ತಿರುಗಿಸಿ)", () => {
    expect(load("kn")["bulk.invert"]).not.toContain("ತಿರುಗಿಸಿ");
  });
  it("kn result.dismiss drops the legalistic 'ವಜಾಗೊಳಿಸಿ'", () => {
    expect(load("kn")["result.dismiss"]).not.toContain("ವಜಾಗೊಳಿಸಿ");
  });
  it("hi result.dismiss drops the harsh 'खारिज'", () => {
    expect(load("hi")["result.dismiss"]).not.toContain("खारिज");
  });
});

### 191. assistant (2026-06-21T19:43:26.580Z)

// @vitest-environment jsdom
//
// Coverage for QueryBar's interaction wiring — the submit handler, the example-chip apply path, the
// live parsed-pill, and the "unreadable" branch were untested (funcs 40%). The offline parser
// (lib/query) is unit-tested separately; here we prove the component glues input → parse → onApply
// honestly, and that the English example chips are hidden for a non-English locale (A9).
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from "@testing-library/react";
import i18n from "../i18n";
import { QueryBar } from "./QueryBar";

beforeEach(async () => {
  window.localStorage.clear();
  await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
});
afterEach(cleanup);

describe("QueryBar", () => {
  const input = () => screen.getByLabelText(/filter rallies/i);
  const applyBtn = () => screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Apply" }) as HTMLButtonElement;

  it("a recognized query shows a parsed pill and submitting applies it as 'typed'", () => {
    const onApply = vi.fn();
    render(<QueryBar onApply={onApply} />);
    fireEvent.change(input(), { target: { value: "over 10s" } });
    expect(screen.getByTestId("query-pill").textContent).toMatch(/length > 10s/); // live interpretation
    expect(applyBtn().disabled).toBe(false);
    fireEvent.click(applyBtn());
    expect(onApply).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(onApply.mock.calls[0][1]).toBe("typed"); // source tag
  });

  it("tapping an example chip fills + applies the query as 'example'", () => {
    const onApply = vi.fn();
    render(<QueryBar onApply={onApply} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "over 10s" }));
    expect(onApply).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(onApply.mock.calls[0][1]).toBe("example");
    expect((input() as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe("over 10s"); // the box is filled with the chip text
  });

  it("an unreadable query shows the 'couldn't read that' hint and keeps Apply disabled", () => {
    const onApply = vi.fn();
    render(<QueryBar onApply={onApply} />);
    fireEvent.change(input(), { target: { value: "10s" } }); // a bare duration with no comparator → not recognized
    expect(screen.getByText(/Couldn't read that/i)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(applyBtn().disabled).toBe(true);
    fireEvent.submit(applyBtn().closest("form")!);
    expect(onApply).not.toHaveBeenCalled(); // submit is a no-op while unrecognized
  });

  it("hides the English-only example chips for a non-English locale (kn)", async () => {
    await i18n.changeLanguage("kn");
    render(<QueryBar onApply={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "over 10s" })).toBeNull();
    await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
  });
});

### 222. assistant (2026-06-21T19:49:08.546Z)

Task complete. Here's the final state.

## Summary

The `#77` catalog fix was already merged last turn, so "ensure coverage is improved and not regressed and merge" meant delivering the coverage discipline as a proper follow-up — which surfaced a key fact: **`web` had no coverage measurement at all** (no provider, no thresholds, CI never gated it). You can't "not regress" what isn't measured, so I stood it up, improved it, and locked it in.

**[khelsutra#80](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/80)** — squash-merged to master (`64076a5`) on green CI (web coverage gate ✓, site ✓, Cloudflare ✓). Branch + worktree cleaned up; master fast-forwarded.

### What landed
- **Coverage tooling + CI floor:** `@vitest/coverage-v8` + a `test:coverage` script + v8 thresholds in `vite.config.ts` (stmts/lines 92, branches 87, funcs 84). CI's web job now runs `npm run test:coverage`, so **a merge can no longer silently drop coverage.**
- **+37 tests (177 → 214):** `SelectionFooter.test.tsx` (the floor-warning block **66% → 100%**, doubling as a #77 render guard), `QueryBar.test.tsx` (handlers **40% → 100% funcs**), and `catalog-invariants.test.ts` (a data-level lock for every #77 bug class).
- **Coverage: 90.44% → 93.34% lines** (funcs 80.45 → 85.88), gate green on CI.

### Rigor
- **Adversarially reviewed** via a multi-agent workflow before merge — it caught (and I reproduced) that my *first* draft of the invariants left the Hindi over-Sanskritization class and 7 of 10 brand keys **unguarded** (the bug could revert with tests staying green). I rewrote the guards to be self-maintaining (derived from en) and **mutation-tested** them: reintroducing each #77 bug — engine jargon (Latin *or* transliterated), the `<code>` token, brand transliteration (incl. a spaced `खेल सूत्र` variant that evaded the original denylist), the Hindi stiff terms — now fails a test.
- **Honest coverage accounting** (in the PR body): the 90.44→93.34 gain is three distinct moves — excluding non-logic files (`main.tsx`, `api/types.ts`) reframes the baseline to ~91.36; the `SelectionFooter`/`QueryBar` tests add the *real* source-line coverage to 93.34; `catalog-invariants.test.ts` adds **zero** source-line coverage (it's a content-regression guard, not part of the ratchet).

### Notes for next time
- Per the concurrency convention, I worked in an isolated worktree and rebased onto `origin/master` twice (it churned: #78/#79 landed mid-task).
- Remaining coverage gap is `App.tsx` (~75%, the engine-orchestrating shell) — a good future ratchet, noted in the handoff.
- Both PRs (#77 catalog fix, #80 coverage) are merged; nothing is left open.
